# Family L aggregate — ELAI deep feature discovery

## Document contract

- **Family:** L (Causal Grounding Gate + Output Quality/Familiarity + Context Core + Measured Competitor/Eval + DeepReason/Exploratory Reasoning; L-0..L-24).
- **Inputs:** `DEEP-L-001`, `DEEP-L-002`, `DEEP-L-003`, and `12-L-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md`.
- **Policy:** Every distinct atomic record is preserved with its ID and source anchor (ledger file + line). No prose compression. No silent drop of minor atoms.
- **Namespacing:** Deep atoms keep native IDs under ledger prefix `L-001/`, `L-002/`, or `L-003/`. Summary atoms use `SUMMARY/`. Cross-file IX/SD/AC stay namespaced by ledger (e.g. `L-001/IX-003`, `L-003/SD-L15-001`).
- **NEWER-PLAN PRECEDENCE (within family):** An explicit SUPERSEDED/replacement/CORRECT marker in a source beats everything; otherwise a higher plan number is newer and wins the **target state** description. Losing descriptions are retained as superseded notes with anchors. Deep ledgers supersede the summary orientation pass where they conflict.
- **L-5 PROVENANCE (binding):** DEEP-L-003 extracted L-5 from the recovered manifest-pinned image (103 ln / sha256 `b254e847…`, hash-verified). The live L-plan file is newer (272 ln / `7d13e7a1…`) and was **NOT** extracted. There is **NO** drifted-content extraction in this aggregate.
- **Status language preserved:** PLANNED / SHIPPED / WIRE-DARK / DARK / PARTIAL / DEFERRED / BENCH-PENDING are not collapsed.
- **Generated:** 2026-08-12 (L-family aggregate rebuild from pin-based DEEP-L-003)

## Notation

- `[E]` / `E/*` explicit · `[I]` inference · `[A]` ambiguity · `[C]` conflict/correction · `[Δ]` summary delta · `[SURFACE]` user-facing · `[superseded]` losing target-state retained.
- Domain tags from deep ledgers (STATUS, CAP, SURFACE, FLOW, …) are preserved in the class field.
- Anchors: `L-N:Lx-Ly` = L-plan originals (L-5 against recovered pin only); `LCGG`/`LOQF` = grounding maps; `12-L:Ln` = summary discovery line.

- **L-001/L18-E001** [E/STATUS] anchor=`L-18:L1-L7` unit=`L-18 — projection corollaries: losslessness + time-travel collapse` (ledger-line: DEEP-L-001.discovery.md:L305): L-18 is L-T3 CONTEXT-CORE; binds CC-1, CC-4; BUILD-compose collapsing four separate mechanisms into parameterizations of the L-16 projection; Wave W2; depends L-16.

- **L-001/L18-E003** [E/DEP] anchor=`L-18:L8-L9, L28-L32` unit=`L-18 — projection corollaries: losslessness + time-travel collapse` (ledger-line: DEEP-L-001.discovery.md:L307): Composes K-12 compact.rs:545 critical-atom gate (re-pointed at view), H-49 session_dag undo-tree + CAS, recover_stubs session_dag.rs:1539 (subsumed), H-49 edit_checkpoints.
- **L-001/L18-E004** [E/CAP] anchor=`L-18:L16-L19` unit=`L-18 — projection corollaries: losslessness + time-travel collapse` (ledger-line: DEEP-L-001.discovery.md:L308): LOSSLESSNESS becomes VIEW assertion (CC-4): check_lossless_compaction re-pointed to assert K-12 critical atoms {kind, value, must_preserve} PRESENT in projected view by exact string/symbol presence, never semantic similarity.
- **L-001/L18-E005** [E/FAIL] anchor=`L-18:L19-L21, L46-L48` unit=`L-18 — projection corollaries: losslessness + time-travel collapse` (ledger-line: DEEP-L-001.discovery.md:L309): Gate can now be FAIL-CLOSED with no risk: nothing is dropped from the log; failed assert means projection budget too tight and must widen, not that data was lost.
- **L-001/L18-E006** [E/LIMIT] anchor=`L-18:L20-L21` unit=`L-18 — projection corollaries: losslessness + time-travel collapse` (ledger-line: DEEP-L-001.discovery.md:L310): K-12 R1 fail-closed flip is satisfied structurally by immutable history.

### Summary end-state architecture

- **SUMMARY/ES-001** [E] (12-L:L22): **Claim trust loop [E].** Any backend output is structurally converted to typed claims; round-trip, oscillation, semantic-entropy, counterfactual, and demarcation evidence feed ClaimRisk; risk selectively buys more evidence; a policy gate then allows, caveats, verifies, blocks, or abstains. Contradicted beliefs are quarantined, while proven resolutions may compound into verified cases. (L:6-79, L:240-259)
- **SUMMARY/ES-002** [E] (12-L:L23): **Generated-output quality loop [E].** Plan-time responsibility limits, generation-time critique/retry, write-time deterministic checks, and audit-time frozen authority enforce maintainability for every model. Rules begin in shadow and earn blocking authority from measured error rates. (L:83-88)
- **SUMMARY/ES-003** [E] (12-L:L24): **Familiar user surface on the same trust engine [E].** Skills, typed rules/hooks, a one-shot builder, artifact execution, MCP access, and receipt verification expose existing machinery without bypassing admission, quality, or autonomy gates. (L:90-112)
- **SUMMARY/ES-004** [E] (12-L:L25): **Immutable context substrate [E].** An append-only transcript is projected through a pure fold into a provider view; a named freeze-epoch state machine controls prefix recomputation; losslessness, time travel, recovery, and ambient recall become projection behavior rather than raw-history mutation. (L:117-204)
- **SUMMARY/ES-005** [E] (12-L:L26): **Measured model orchestration [E].** Real commits supply contamination-resistant eval tasks; ELAI and vendor CLI arms run under one reproducible envelope; model capability and role deterministically select verification intensity, with unknown models receiving the strongest checks. (L:206-226)
- **SUMMARY/ES-006** [E] (12-L:L27): **Exploratory reasoning loop [E].** Generators attach executable falsifiers to conjectures, exploration returns rival explanations rather than one collapsed answer, an embedding-free orbit detector rotates stance, and a lane-isolated conjecture store preserves unresolved ideas without contaminating proven-only code work. (L:228-262)
- **SUMMARY/ES-007** [E] (12-L:L28): **Planning/runtime boundary [E].** The separate improvement-conjecture archive has no FDL controller, ProbeUtility scheduler, multi-cycle operator allocator, or bridge into L-23/L-24. Q-10 only proposes a future one-way SOURCE_ONLY research packet under independent target-side verification. (O:393-408)

### Summary workflows

- **SUMMARY/WF-001** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L530): [Claim extraction, evidence, effort, and action] A backend emits an answer/plan/patch/explanation. L-1 structurally extracts typed claims, retains unresolved references, and adds taint. (L:11-16)
- **SUMMARY/WF-002** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L531): [Claim extraction, evidence, effort, and action] L-3 establishes P1 by re-grounding claims; J-85 supplies P2; L-4 establishes P3 from meaning clusters and a cross-family subset. L-5 supplies stronger interventional evidence for causal/behavior claims, and L-22 adds the falsifier/demarcation signal. (L:18-44, L:240-245)
- **SUMMARY/WF-003** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L532): [Claim extraction, evidence, effort, and action] L-2 fuses independent evidence. Cheap signals gate expensive checks; one signal cannot exceed Medium; two distinct signals may reach High/Critical. (L:18-23)
- **SUMMARY/WF-004** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L533): [Claim extraction, evidence, effort, and action] L-6 spends targeted retrieval/test/trace/escalation effort only on High/Critical claims, then loops back to L-2. (L:46-51)
- **SUMMARY/WF-005** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L534): [Claim extraction, evidence, effort, and action] L-7 maps the final tier to allow, caveat/citation, forced verification, block, or abstain and journals the gate. (L:53-58)
- **SUMMARY/WF-006** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L535): [Claim extraction, evidence, effort, and action] L-8 removes later-refuted assumptions from live working belief without erasing evidence. A grounded closure may mint J-96 verified memory; unresolved exploratory conjectures instead remain in the separate L-24 store. (L:48, L:60-65, L:254-259)
- **SUMMARY/WF-007** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L539): [Generated code from idea to verified artifact] L-14 takes an idea through PIPELINE FACTORY and E-12. (L:104-108)
- **SUMMARY/WF-008** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L540): [Generated code from idea to verified artifact] L-11 applies plan-time, generation-time, write-time, and audit-time quality seams. Weak lanes can receive stronger L-21 retry/check/reviewer settings. (L:83-88, L:221-226)
- **SUMMARY/WF-009** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L541): [Generated code from idea to verified artifact] Failed quality reports findings and cannot become DONE; success is wrapped in an RRA receipt and may run under Tier-C or through MCP. (L:104-109)
- **SUMMARY/WF-010** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L542): [Generated code from idea to verified artifact] The same user-created skills and rules enter through L-12/L-13 admission and safety gates; none can bypass immutable checks. (L:90-102)
- **SUMMARY/WF-011** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L546): [Immutable context projection and recall] L-16 appends all history to SessionDag/CAS and derives each model view through pure fold. (L:133-150)
- **SUMMARY/WF-012** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L547): [Immutable context projection and recall] L-17 reuses the frozen prefix unless one of seven named causes triggers a journaled epoch recompute. Tail growth alone does not move the prefix. (L:152-164)
- **SUMMARY/WF-013** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L548): [Immutable context projection and recall] L-18 verifies critical atoms in the derived view, widening the budget on failure, and implements undo/redo/branch/recovery as projection at another index or budget. (L:166-178)
- **SUMMARY/WF-014** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L549): [Immutable context projection and recall] L-19 appends recall cards to the tail under pressure-sensitive budgets and expires/refolds them by TTL. (L:180-193)
- **SUMMARY/WF-015** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L553): [Evaluation and claims about value] L-20 mines real commits into hashed, base-SHA-bound tasks, then runs ELAI and competitor CLIs under an identical envelope. (L:214-219)
- **SUMMARY/WF-016** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L554): [Evaluation and claims about value] L-21 varies verification effort by model capability/role while L-20/K-15 measures verified outcomes and tokens per correct. (L:221-226)
- **SUMMARY/WF-017** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L555): [Evaluation and claims about value] L-10 independently ablates the hallucination gate. L-24 extends its explore arm with a grounding/retrieval novelty matrix. (L:74-79, L:256)
- **SUMMARY/WF-018** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L556): [Evaluation and claims about value] Public comparative/value claims remain absent until the appropriate ELAI-measured paired deltas exist. No paper metric, live-cost estimate, or leaderboard language may substitute. (L:76, L:106, L:216, L:219)
- **SUMMARY/WF-019** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L560): [Exploratory reasoning and isolated conjecture memory] L-22 elicits candidate conjectures and their executable falsifiers in one grammar-constrained generation call. (L:240-245)
- **SUMMARY/WF-020** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L561): [Exploratory reasoning and isolated conjecture memory] L-23 iterates rival conjectures, gate-side refutation memory, stance rotation, orbit detection, turnover, and cross-family survivor judgment. (L:247-252)
- **SUMMARY/WF-021** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L562): [Exploratory reasoning and isolated conjecture memory] L-24 stores unresolved/refuted/suspended conjectures with computed lifecycle, missing tests, provenance, and evaluator edges. (L:254-259)
- **SUMMARY/WF-022** [E] [workflow] (12-L:L563): [Exploratory reasoning and isolated conjecture memory] Only the explore lane may retrieve unproven conjectures. Verified Case Graph remains proven-only, and refuted content influences the gate rather than the generation prompt. (L:256-257)

### Summary invariants / constraints

- **SUMMARY/INV-001** [E] [invariant] label=`HG-1 causal means interventional` (12-L:L567): HG-1 causal means interventional. Static, temporal, import-edge, or rubric evidence cannot set causal_supported; only an executable perturb-and-observe pass can. (L:41-44, L:245)
- **SUMMARY/INV-002** [E] [invariant] label=`HG-2 convergence, not one signal` (12-L:L568): HG-2 convergence, not one signal. High/Critical needs at least two mechanistically distinct predictors; one predictor caps at Medium. (L:20, L:23)
- **SUMMARY/INV-003** [E] [invariant] label=`HG-3 black-box` (12-L:L569): HG-3 black-box. Claims are extracted outside the model and risk scoring must work without logprobs on any backend. (L:13, L:20, L:23)
- **SUMMARY/INV-004** [E] [invariant] label=`HG-4 abstain or verify` (12-L:L570): HG-4 abstain or verify. Missing/unverifiable High evidence never silently passes; unchecked math is abstained. (L:55-58, L:69-72)
- **SUMMARY/INV-005** [E] [invariant] label=`HG-5 demarcation` (12-L:L571): HG-5 demarcation. A Causal/Behavior claim with no falsifier has a High floor, and a hypothesis cannot converge without one. (L:242-245)
- **SUMMARY/INV-006** [E] [invariant] label=`GD-1 compose, do not restate` (12-L:L572): GD-1 compose, do not restate. L repeatedly forbids duplicated grounding, mutation, runners, routing, critic, hook, skill, and case-graph machinery. (L:14, L:28, L:42, L:77, L:93, L:100, L:107, L:217, L:224, L:243, L:250, L:257)
- **SUMMARY/INV-007** [E] [invariant] label=`GD-2 reproducibility` (12-L:L573): GD-2 reproducibility. Risk, gates, context views, lifecycle, fixture outputs, and measurements derive from journaled data and ELAI_SEED. (L:23, L:55, L:65, L:76, L:88, L:95, L:102, L:147-150, L:203-204, L:226, L:252, L:259)
- **SUMMARY/INV-008** [E] [invariant] label=`GD-3 receipts, not proxy prestige` (12-L:L574): GD-3 receipts, not proxy prestige. Measurement axes use receipts/verified outcomes rather than pass@k or leaderboard rank. (L:76, L:216, L:219)
- **SUMMARY/INV-009** [E] [invariant] label=`GD-4 observe first` (12-L:L575): GD-4 observe first. Hallucination policy, quality rules, and verification intensity must bound error/over-block behavior before active blocking. (L:55, L:85, L:99, L:115, L:223)
- **SUMMARY/INV-010** [E] [invariant] label=`GD-5 honest residue` (12-L:L576): GD-5 honest residue. The claim gate catches coverage-gap hallucination, not all confidently shared wrong beliefs; cross-family evidence is mitigation, not elimination. (L:7, L:34, L:115)
- **SUMMARY/INV-011** [E] [invariant] label=`TD-3 deterministic authority` (12-L:L577): TD-3 deterministic authority. LLM judgments, typicality, hard-commit screening, and reviewers remain advisory where stated; pure tables/checkers/gates own authoritative verdicts. (L:34-35, L:55, L:69, L:85, L:92, L:99, L:216, L:223, L:242)
- **SUMMARY/INV-012** [E] [invariant] label=`R8 separation/monotonicity` (12-L:L578): R8 separation/monotonicity. The claimant/executor cannot self-grade; immutable checks cannot be weakened; policy rows and user rules may only add verification. (L:13, L:55, L:85, L:99, L:223)
- **SUMMARY/INV-013** [E] [invariant] label=`CC-1 immutable raw history` (12-L:L579): CC-1 immutable raw history. Raw transcript bytes are append-only and attempted mutation fails. (L:123-129, L:135-150)
- **SUMMARY/INV-014** [E] [invariant] label=`CC-2 pure view/prefix stability` (12-L:L580): CC-2 pure view/prefix stability. Identical fold inputs produce byte-identical views; recall and tail growth do not rewrite the frozen prefix. (L:127-129, L:147-150, L:152-164, L:180-193)
- **SUMMARY/INV-015** [E] [invariant] label=`CC-3 named epoch causes` (12-L:L581): CC-3 named epoch causes. Every prefix change must have one enumerated, journaled cause; unexplained change hard-fails. (L:154-164)
- **SUMMARY/INV-016** [E] [invariant] label=`CC-4 structural losslessness` (12-L:L582): CC-4 structural losslessness. Critical atoms exact-match in all later views; too-tight budgets widen instead of losing history. (L:168-178)
- **SUMMARY/INV-017** [E] [invariant] label=`EX-1 negative case stays gate-side` (12-L:L583): EX-1 negative case stays gate-side. Refuted/tabu ideas never appear in the conjecture prompt. (L:249, L:252, L:257)
- **SUMMARY/INV-018** [E] [invariant] label=`EX-2 no embedding orbit detector` (12-L:L584): EX-2 no embedding orbit detector. Orbiting is measured by gate-block rate, not vector similarity. (L:249, L:252)
- **SUMMARY/INV-019** [E] [invariant] label=`EX-3 preserve rival explanations` (12-L:L585): EX-3 preserve rival explanations. A single collapsed answer fails the explore contract. (L:252)
- **SUMMARY/INV-020** [E] [invariant] label=`EX-5 lane isolation` (12-L:L586): EX-5 lane isolation. Any unproven ConjectureNode returned to code-fix/auditor retrieval fails. (L:256, L:259)
- **SUMMARY/INV-021** [E] [invariant] label=`EX-6 computed lifecycle` (12-L:L587): EX-6 computed lifecycle. Hand-set status fails; never-criticized cannot be live. (L:256, L:259)
- **SUMMARY/INV-022** [E] [invariant] label=`No deletion of contradicted knowledge` (12-L:L588): No deletion of contradicted knowledge. Working beliefs and conjectures are quarantined, never erased. (L:62-65, L:256)
- **SUMMARY/INV-023** [E] [invariant] label=`No fabricated measurements` (12-L:L589): No fabricated measurements. Live metrics refuse to emit before their live arm runs; external single-seed/paper numbers are rationale only. (L:76, L:79, L:262)
